PathBuf::from(\"./target/debug/bf\"))\n24\t}\n25\t\n26\t/// Create a temporary workspace for storage-level tests\n27\tfn setup_storage_workspace() -> (TempDir, PathBuf) {\n28\t    let temp_dir = &config_path,\n37\t        r#\"issue_prefixes: [bf]\n38\tdefault_priority: 2\n39\tdefault_type: task\n40\tclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n41\t\"#,\n42\t    )\n43\t    .unwrap();\n44\t\n45\t    let )\n50\t    .unwrap();\n51\t\n52\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n53\t    Storage::open(&db_path).unwrap();\n54\t\n55\t    (temp_dir, beads_dir)\n56\t}\n57\t\n58\t/// Initialize a test .expect(\"Failed to initialize workspace\");\n71\t\n72\t    assert!(\n73\t        init_result.status.success(),\n74\t        \"bf init failed: {}\",\n75\t
